country_event = {
	id = can_dnc_a.1
	title = can_dnc_a.1.t
	desc = can_dnc_a.1.d
	is_triggered_only = yes

		name = can_dnc_a.1.a
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = can_dnc_a.2
	title = can_dnc_a.2.t
	desc = can_dnc_a.2.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = can_dnc_a.2.a
		ai_chance = {
			base = 60
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				FROM = {
					has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_a_fortresses_offered
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_a_answered_by_silence
				}
			}
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = FROM
			modifier = RI_supports_own_country
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_a.3 hours = 6 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = can_dnc_a.2.b
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_a.4 hours = 6 }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = can_dnc_a.2.c
		ai_chance = {
			base = 15
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				FROM = {
					has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_a_answered_by_silence
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.5
				FROM = {
					has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_a_fortresses_offered
				}
			}
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = {
			target = FROM
			modifier = RI_trade_agreement_declined
		}
		FROM = {
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_a.5 hours = 6 }
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = can_dnc_b.40
	title = can_dnc_b.40.t
	desc = can_dnc_b.40.d
	picture = GFX_report_event_canada_army
	trigger = {
		tag = CAN
		has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_path_imperial
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_b_kings_war_called
		}
		is_subject_of = ENG
		ENG = {
			has_war = yes
			any_enemy_country = { is_major = yes }
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 5 }
	option = {
		name = can_dnc_b.40.a
		ai_chance = { base = 60 }
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = CAN_dnc_b_kings_war_called
			days = 730
			value = 1
		}
		add_war_support = 0.05
		add_stability = -0.05
		army_experience = 20
		add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_fracture = 2 }
		clamp_variable = {
			var = CAN_dnc_fracture
			min = 0
			max = 10
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = CANDNC_b_fracture_plus_two_tt
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_template = "Rifle Battalion" }
			}
			division_template = {
				name = "Rifle Battalion"
				division_names_group = CAN_INF_01

	}

		text = can_dnc_c.50.d.no
		trigger = { has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_c_vote_no }
	}
	picture = GFX_report_event_election_vote
	is_triggered_only = yes
	fire_only_once = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = 40 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_american_west }
				add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = 20 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_us_reciprocity }
				add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = 10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_us_guarantee }
				add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = 10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_ontario_loyalists_roused
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = 15 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CAN_dnc_loyalist_anger > 3 }
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = 10 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = CAN_dnc_c_yes_bonus }
				add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share = CAN_dnc_c_yes_bonus }
			}
			clamp_variable = {
				var = CAN_dnc_c_yes_share
				min = 0
				max = 100
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { CAN_dnc_c_yes_share > 49 }
				}
				set_country_flag = CAN_dnc_c_vote_yes
			}
			else = { set_country_flag = CAN_dnc_c_vote_no }
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = can_dnc_c.50.a
		trigger = { has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_c_vote_yes }
		add_political_power = 50
		add_stability = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = can_dnc_c.50.share_tt
	}

		name = can_dnc_c.50.b
		trigger = { has_country_flag = CAN_dnc_c_vote_no }
		add_political_power = -50
		add_stability = -0.05
		custom_effect_tooltip = can_dnc_c.50.share_tt

		}
	}

	title = can_dnc_d.65.t
	desc = can_dnc_d.65.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = can_dnc_d.65.a
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
		add_war_support = 0.05
		add_stability = -0.03
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = large_decrease }
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = CAN_d_the_preference_kept }
			remove_ideas = CAN_d_the_preference_kept
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = CAN_d_no_credit_in_london }
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = CAN_d_no_credit_in_london
				add_idea = CAN_d_no_credit_anywhere
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_idea = CAN_d_no_credit_in_london }
				NOT = { has_idea = CAN_d_no_credit_anywhere }
			}
			add_ideas = CAN_d_no_credit_anywhere
		}
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = can_dnc_e.53
	title = can_dnc_e.53.t
	desc = can_dnc_e.53.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes
	option = {
		name = can_dnc_e.53.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { country_exists = QUE }
			}
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_e.54 days = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_war_with = QUE }
			}
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_e.55 days = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				468 = { is_controlled_by = CAN }
				842 = { is_controlled_by = CAN }
			}
			annex_country = {
				target = QUE
				transfer_troops = no
			}
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_e.54 days = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { CAN_dnc_e_war_ticks > 23 }
			}
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_e.55 days = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { CAN_dnc_e_war_ticks = 1 }
			country_event = { id = can_dnc_e.53 days = 10 }
		}
	}
}

			multiply_variable = { CAN_dnc_fracture_cons = -0.01 }
			set_variable = { CAN_dnc_anger_pp = CAN_dnc_loyalist_anger }
			multiply_variable = { CAN_dnc_anger_pp = -0.015 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CAN_dnc_two_nations_modifier }
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = CAN_dnc_two_nations_modifier }
			}
		}
